I mapped the mission screen in /Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w14/apps/mobile (read-only). Paths below are under `lib/` unless they start with `test/` or `packages/`. Two of your premises don't match the code: no screen draws a second interviewer (section 4), and the mission UI has no XP and no "tempting" result (section 3).

## 1. State machine (`features/mission/mission_player.dart`)

**Stages.** `SceneStage` (:32) runs card → opening → lines → push → draft → beats → followUp → replies → debrief → epilogue. `_go` never moves backwards (:236). Inside `beats` there is a second machine, `BeatPhase` (:66): cards → why → reasons → reaction.

**How the screen is stacked** (`build`, :567):
- Background: `ColoredBox(BrandVivid.night)`, then `FilmOverlay(grain:0, vignetteColor: grade.shadow)`.
- Camera: `HardCut(shot:_tight)` wraps `DollyCamera(closeUp:_closeUp, onPushEnd:_pushDone, layers: MissionSet.layers(...))` (:623-644).
- Then, on top: `BeatTimer`, `SceneScrim`, a full-screen tap layer labelled `MissionPlayer.continueLabel`, and `AnimatedLetterbox(color: BrandVivid.night)`.
- `_Overlay` (:906) holds the `TrustMeter`, the `GuessCue` and the `LowerThird`. The lower third starts at `regionTop = MissionSet.faceBottom(...) + BrandSpace.sm` (:576).
- `LeaveControl` and `LeaveSheet` sit last.

**Each stage** (the lower-third content comes from `_lowerThird`, :730):
- **card**: `MissionCard` inside a Semantics button labelled `cardContinueLabel` (:690). Tapping it runs `_cardDone`, which starts the ambience sound and shows the letterbox bars.
- **opening**: the letterbox only. `_barsSettled` then `_startLines` calls `controller.sceneStarted()`.
- **lines / push**: `TypewriterSubtitle` (speaker, line, grade, controller `_typer`) wrapped in `IgnorePointer`, plus `NextControl` with the auto-advance countdown. When the question line finishes, `_closeUp=true` pushes the dolly in, and `_pushDone` removes the bars.
- **draft**: `QuestionReminder` and `DraftPanel(onDone)`. That calls `controller.finishDraft(String?)`.
- **beats / cards**: `QuestionReminder` plus `AnswerCards<AnswerOption>` (key `beat-$i`, label from `beatLabel(...)` or `changeHint`). A built beat shows `BuildPanel` instead (key `build-$i`).
- **why**: a `TypewriterSubtitle` of `beat.why!.line`. When it finishes, `_whyTyped` moves to `reasons`.
- **reasons**: the subtitle plus `AnswerCards<Reason>` (key `why-$i`, label `reasonLabel`). Picking calls `controller.giveReason(Reason)`.
- **reaction**: a `TypewriterSubtitle` of `_reaction`. It holds 1400 ms or until a tap (:505-516).
- **followUp / replies**: a hard cut (`_tight`), the subtitle `game.followUpLine`, then `AnswerCards<AnswerOption>` (key `'replies'`, label `replyLabel`). Picking calls `controller.answerFollowUp(option)`.
- **debrief**: `DebriefView` (:579). **epilogue**: `EpilogueScene` (:599, `features/mission/scene/epilogue_scene.dart`).

**All controller calls:** `sceneStarted()`, `finishDraft(String?)`, `lock(AnswerOption, {Duration? decidedIn})`, `lockBuilt(List<String> placed, {decidedIn})`, `giveReason(Reason)`, `timerExpired()`, `answerFollowUp(AnswerOption)` (async), `leave()`. From `features/mission/mission_screen.dart:72-79`: `resume()`, `startAfresh()`.

**What the UI reads from `MissionState`** (`features/mission/mission_controller.dart:63-216`): `mission`, `scene`, `question`, `playerName`, `plays`, `resumed`, `saved`, `draftDone`, `followUp`, `reply`, `score`, `trust`, `beatIndex`, `beatsDone`, `awaitingWhy`, `optionsForBeat(i)`, `fragmentsForBeat(i)`, `reasonsForBeat(i)`, `qualityOf(i)`, `reactionFor(i, q)`, `replyOptions`, `followUpLine`, `firstTry`, `baselineDraft`.

**Interviewer face.** `_expression` (:867) chooses smile, neutral, thinking, nod or hmm depending on stage and phase.

**Tokens and widgets in use:**
- Colours: `BrandVivid.night/accent/onDeep/onDeepSoft/horizon/hairline()/cardShadow/glowShadow()`.
- Type: `BrandVividType.kicker/row/body/title`, plus `BrandType.choice/eyebrow/score/titleCard/display`.
- `BrandColors.textSecondary/textPrimary/void_`.
- Chapter colours: `grade.glow/key/tint/shadow/deep/deeper/cardGradient`.
- Film kit (`ui/film_kit.dart`): `FilmOverlay`, `HardCut`, `DollyCamera`, `AnimatedLetterbox`, `TypewriterSubtitle`, `TypewriterController`, `SpeechPanel`, `VividBackdrop`, `Spotlight`, `InterviewerSilhouette`, `Timeline`, `cue`, `mix`, `Pressable`, `VividButton`, `stampThud`.

## 2. Answer options

**`AnswerCards<T>`** (`features/mission/scene/lower_third.dart:89`):
- Parameters: `label`, `items`, `textOf`, `grade`, `onPick`, `selected?`, `locked?`, `onArrived?`.
- The label is a `BrandVividType.kicker` in `grade.glow`.
- The cards arrive in a staggered `Timeline(BrandMotion.cinematic)` (stagger 0.16, scale from 0.97).
- State mapping (:116): if something is locked, the locked item shows `locked` and the rest `disabled`; otherwise `selected` or `idle`.

**`ChoiceCard`** (`features/mission/widgets/choice_card.dart:13`):
- Parameters: `numeral`, `text`, `grade`, `state` (an `AnswerCardState`), `onTap`.
- Look: radius 18, `grade.cardGradient`. When lit (selected or locked) it gets a 2px `BrandVivid.accent` ring and `glowShadow`. Locked adds a 4px accent bar on the left. Disabled drops to 0.4 opacity. It is not tappable once locked or disabled.

**`AnswerCard`** (`ui/choice/answer_card.dart:31`) is a near-copy with a `focused` state and `minHeight 48`. It owns `AnswerCardState` (idle, focused, selected, locked, disabled; :7) and `numeralFor(i)`.

**Labels are roman numerals ("i.", "ii.", …), not letters** (:51-53). They sit in a 30px column in `BrandVividType.row` italic. The Daylight `PickCard` (`ui/daylight/pick_card.dart:26`) already takes `letter`, `icon`, `lockedIcon` and `PickState`.

**Lock-in:**
- The first tap selects and starts a 2 s `changeWindow` timer. While it runs, the label switches to `changeHint`.
- A second tap on the same card locks immediately; tapping another card changes the pick (`_pick`, :359).
- `_say` (:376) plays the sound and `stampThud`, sets `_locked`, then calls `lock` (or `answerFollowUp` for a reply).
- Built beats instead use `BuildPanel.lockIn` ("Lock it in", a `VividButton`) → `_lockBuilt` (:406).
- `BuildPanel` / `FragmentChip` are in `features/mission/widgets/build_panel.dart:15,121`. Chip parameters: `text`, `grade`, `position?`, `onTap`.

## 3. "Why that line?" and the result

- The why line is a `TypewriterSubtitle`, followed by `AnswerCards<Reason>` (`features/mission/mission_player.dart:818-835`).
- `Reason` (`packages/content/lib/src/mission.dart:281`) has `id`, `text` and `soundFor: Set<String>`. **There are no icons.**
- One tap locks a reason: `selected` and `locked` are both `_reason`.
- How a pick landed is `PickQuality { strong, weak, guessed }` (`packages/engine/lib/src/beat.dart:72`). **"Tempting" doesn't exist.**
- A guess (`isGuess(decidedIn)`) shows `GuessCue` ("TOO FAST: READ BEFORE YOU ANSWER", `features/mission/widgets/scene_notes.dart:9`) for 2.6 s under the meter. She then reacts with the guessed line before the why step (:421-440).
- Otherwise the result is only her typed reaction line (`reactionFor`), her face (`_expression`), the trust-meter jolt and a sound.
- **There is no feedback panel and no XP anywhere in `features/mission`.** The Daylight `FeedbackSheet` (`ui/daylight/feedback_sheet.dart:25`) has `tone`, `title`, `note`, `xp`, `lesson`, `actionLabel` and `onAction`.

## 4. How the interviewer is drawn — no second interviewer

- `MissionSet.layers` (`features/mission/scene/mission_set.dart:127`) builds three `CameraLayer`s: `VividBackdrop`, `_Interviewer` (one figure) and the desk taken from `BoardroomSet.layers(...)[2]`.
- The player passes only `mission.lead`, which is `interviewerById(question.speakerId)` (`features/mission/mission_lookup.dart:34`; `features/mission/mission_player.dart:636`). The voice player does the same (`features/voice/voice_player.dart:395-397`).
- The panel mission m07 (Priya and Marcus) shows **one figure**. The second interviewer appears only as the speaker name on subtitles.
- `BoardroomSet._Middle` (`ui/set/boardroom_set.dart:83-127`) and the Daylight `RoomStage` (`ui/daylight/room_stage.dart:83-93`, one `CastFigure` at `Alignment(0.08, 1)`) are also single-figure. There is no second-figure offset in this worktree, so the overlap must be in your new design or another branch.

**The geometry the lead uses**, which a second figure would have to avoid:
- `figureWidth = 0.5` and `sunk = 0.12` (:60, :63).
- `figureRect` = `Rect.fromLTWH((w-fw)/2, deskY - h + h*sunk, fw, h)` with `deskY = height * 0.68` (:70-75).
- `faceInDesign = Rect.fromLTRB(29,18,72,69)` (:67).
- Close shots are the kit's shots lifted by `_lift = 0.31` (:33). `tight = CameraShot(scale:2, focus:(0.03,-0.12), target:(0,-0.6))` (:47).
- The kit's `middleClose` is scale 1.75, focus (0,-0.02), target (0,-0.36) (`ui/set/boardroom_set.dart:30`).
- At scale 1.75–2, a centred figure 0.5× wide fills about 90–100% of the screen width. Any second figure placed beside it inside the same middle layer would either be cut off or overlap the lead's face.
- `faceRect` / `faceBottom` (:99-123) only know about the lead's face. `regionTop` and `SceneScrim` are derived from it.

## 5. Mission title card

- `MissionCard` (`features/mission/scene/mission_card.dart:26-36`):
  - Eyebrow: `CHAPTER ONE` or `CHAPTER ONE  ·  MISSION n`.
  - Title: `(opens ? chapter.title : mission.title).toUpperCase()`.
  - Subtitle: `Mission n: …` or `with Priya, the …`.
- `ChapterTitleCard` (`ui/title/chapter_title_card.dart`):
  - Layout: `SafeArea(minimum: all(24))` → `BrassFramePainter` → `MediaQuery.withClampedTextScaling(maxScaleFactor: 1.5)` → `SingleChildScrollView(padding: all(24))` → `Transform.scale(mix(1.06, 1, titleT))` (:104-114).
  - Title: `TrackedTitle(text, style: BrandType.titleCard, t)` (:133). Subtitle: `BrandType.display` at fontSize 20 (:148).
- `BrandType.titleCard` (`packages/brand/lib/src/typography.dart:33`): `fontSize: 40, height: 1.15, letterSpacing: 7, w500`, display serif. **The size is fixed.**
- `TrackedTitle` (`ui/title/tracked_title.dart`):
  - Letter spacing animates from `defaultTrackFrom = 22` to 7 (:25, :50).
  - Lines are broken greedily, between words only, measured at the final spacing (:125-140).
  - Each line is drawn with `maxLines:1, softWrap:false, overflow: visible` inside an `OverflowBox` (:58-72).
  - The whole title shrinks only if a single word is wider than the box (:113-122).
- **Why it wraps badly at 411px:** the usable width is 411 − 48 − 48 = **315px**. 40px capitals plus 7px spacing come to about 30–35px per letter, so roughly 9–10 letters per line.
  - "TELL ME ABOUT YOURSELF" becomes three ragged lines (TELL ME / ABOUT / YOURSELF), with no line balancing.
  - "THE EXPERIENCE GAP": the word EXPERIENCE alone is about 340px, so the entire title shrinks.
  - During the track-in at 22 spacing, each line overflows the frame on both sides, and the 1.06 scale adds to it.

| Widget | File | Parameters / notes |
|---|---|---|
| `TrustMeter` | `features/mission/widgets/trust_meter.dart:15` | `trust` (0–100), `grade`. Height 28, label "TRUST". Heartbeat painter colour lerps from `BrandVivid.horizon` to `grade.glow`. Placed at top-left, 0.55 of screen width (`features/mission/mission_player.dart:927-941`). |
| `BeatTimer` | `features/mission/widgets/beat_timer.dart:9` | `builder(ctx, dim, secondsLeft?)`, `onExpired`, `onPulse({finalSeconds})`, `running`, `duration` (60 s). Shown only on timed missions, outside calm mode, in beats/cards. `_TimerShade` dims the scene with night up to 0.55 and shows the number in `BrandType.score` under the leave control (:972). |

**There is no beat-progress widget.** Progress shows only as label text such as "OPEN  ·  1 OF 3" (`beatLabel` / `buildLabel`, `features/mission/scene/lower_third.dart:58-71`). The Daylight kit has `BeatProgress(beats, current, progress)` and `TrustPips(trust, icon, max=5)`.

**By type:** `ChoiceCard` (in nearly every file, plus `widgetWithText(ChoiceCard, text)` in `mission_harness.dart:435,463`), `FragmentChip`, `BuildPanel`, `MissionCard`, `ChapterTitleCard`, `NextControl`, `TypewriterSubtitle`, `TypewriterText`, `QuestionReminder`, `SpeechPanel`, `LowerThird`, `TrustMeter`, `BeatTimer`, `InterviewerSilhouette`, `DollyCamera`, `LeaveSheet`, `BlockSemantics`, `SingleChildScrollView` / `Scrollable` (ancestors of a card), `MissionPlayer`, `EpilogueScene`.

**By text / constant:** `beatLabel(...)`, `buildLabel(1, n)`, `reasonLabel`, `changeHint`, `GuessCue.text.toUpperCase()`, `BuildPanel.empty/hint/lockIn`, `DraftPanel.skip`, `LeaveSheet.title/resume/leave`, `ResumeSheet.title/resume/restart`, `textContaining('NEXT')`, `'·  1'`, `'·  '`, `NextControl.pausedLabel.toUpperCase()`. Timer numbers are found as `Text` widgets parsed to int ≤ 10 (`timed_mission_test.dart:25`).

**By semantics label:** `RegExp('^Next')`, `LeaveControl.label`.

**Geometry (fragile under a restyle):**
- `owner_fixes_test.dart:20-35, 103-104`: face (`MissionSet.faceInDesign/faceRect/faceBottom`) vs `QuestionReminder` / `LowerThird`.
- `owner_fixes_test.dart:171-180`: `DollyCamera` must fill the screen; `TrustMeter.top ≥ insets.top`; `LowerThird.bottom` within the safe area.

## 8. Chapter and voice

- `Mission.chapter` is an `int` (`packages/content/lib/src/mission.dart:50`).
- `mission.grade` is an extension getter, `ChapterGrade.values[chapter - 1]` (`features/mission/mission_lookup.dart:37`). The same file has `opensChapter`, `closesChapter`, `leadsToReport`, `offersDraft` (= `!hinted`) and `lead`. `Mission.timed` is a field.
- **There is no `isVoice`.** Voice missions use `mission.answerMode == AnswerMode.voice` (`packages/content/lib/src/mission.dart:99,107`) plus `mission.voice` (`VoicePrompt?`). `features/mission/mission_screen.dart:41` routes them to `VoicePlayer`, so the mission player only ever plays choice missions.

- **Controller methods:** `sceneStarted()` :170, `answered(Take?)` :181, `replied(Take?)` :188 (which calls `_score()` :206), `selfChecked(Set<String>)` :251, `_complete()` :261 and `discardTakes()` :331.
- **Mic states don't live in the controller.** They come from `TakeRecorder.phase` (a ChangeNotifier). `RecorderPhase` covers checking/explain/asking/denied/empty/recording/paused/full (`recorder/take_recorder.dart:11-36`), plus `PauseReason` player/silence (:39-45). Getters are at :107-118. Methods: `start` :144, `allow` :156, `toggle` :175, `resume` :182, `pause` :236, `askDelete` :263, `cancelDelete` :274, `confirmDelete` :280, `submit` :292.
- **Screen-level stage** is `VoiceStage` (`voice_player.dart:30-54`). `build` checks in this order (:322-369): epilogue, then `score != null` → VoiceDebrief, then `selfCheck` → SelfCheckPanel, then `scoring` → ListeningCard, then the scene.

**Voice debrief: why the note and the red-pen dimension collide**

How the dossier draws them:
- The sheet is `Dossier(note: lesson?.title, markedIndex: _marked)` (`voice_debrief.dart:115-136`).
- In `lib/ui/paper/dossier.dart`, the underline is `PenStroke.underline` drawn in `Positioned.fill` over the marked ledger label (:290-293).
  - Colour: `BrandColors.redPen` #B83A2A, 1.4px (`ui/paper/pen.dart:12-13`).
  - Path: from x=-2 to 0.75×width (pen.dart:24-33). Because the Stack is inside `Expanded`, that is 75% of the label column, not the word's width.
- The note is in the bottom Row (:234-248): `Expanded(PenNote)`, then 8px, then `StampIn(WaxSeal 84px, angle -0.12)`.
  - `PenNote` uses `BrandType.note`: display italic 24/1.1 in redPen (`packages/brand/lib/src/typography.dart:139-147`). It is rotated -0.05 rad about centerLeft (pen.dart:110,119-121).
  - The seal is lerped from redPen (`wax_seal.dart:93`).
  - The Row is cross-axis centred. Above it there is only `md` (16px) and the `DoubleRule` (:232-233).

What collides:
1. **Different meaning.**
   - The text debrief derives both marks from one lesson: `markedDimensionIndex = lesson.flag.lowers` (`features/debrief/debrief_facts.dart:53-57`), and both are null on a clean run.
   - The voice debrief takes the note from the mission's single `voice.lessonId` (`voice_debrief.dart:92,128`). The underline comes separately from the costliest **missed point's** `p.dimension` (`_marked`, :76-85).
   - So the red margin note can name a lesson about one dimension while the red underline sits on another. That gives two red-pen marks saying different things.
2. **Red pen on a perfect run.** If every point was made, `_marked` is null but `note` is still the lesson title. The pen still writes, and its cue still fires (`dossier.dart:120-121`). The result is a red correction with nothing underlined, while `VoiceLessonSlip` hides the flaw (`missedAny: _marked != null`, :181).
3. **The same title twice.** The lesson title appears again as a 24px display heading in `VoiceLessonSlip` (:457-465).
4. **Two different reds.**
   - The dossier uses `BrandColors.redPen` #B83A2A.
   - The slips' "red pen" is `SlipInk.mark` = `BrandVivid.horizon`, #F2994A orange (`red_pen_slip.dart:181-182`). It is used for missed points (:391,399) and the lesson flaw (:471).
   - So the underlined dimension and the missed points it refers to are in different colours. The red-pen note also sits next to the same-hue oxblood seal.
5. **Possible physical overlap (my geometry estimate; I didn't render it).**
   - At the clamped 1.5x text scale (`DebriefView.maxSheetTextScale`), a two-line note is about 79px tall against the 84px seal, so the centring leaves almost no slack.
   - The -0.05 rotation then lifts its right end about 10px, into the 16px gap and toward the DoubleRule and the last ledger row's underline.

## B. Recall

**States** (`lib/features/recall/recall_controller.dart`; sealed `RecallRoomState`, :76-137):
- `RecallLoading` → "Opening the room…"
- `RecallEmpty(hasCards)`: false gives "Nothing to recall yet", true gives "All caught up".
- `RecallUnavailable` → "Try again", which calls `ref.invalidate`.
- `RecallPlaying(round, index, verdicts, chosenId)`: `answered`, `verdict`, `streak`, `progress`.
- `RecallFinished(verdicts)`: `known`, `bestStreak`.

`recall_screen.dart:48-78` maps them. Every non-card state uses `_Message` (:148-174): `VividPanel(ChapterGrade.pressure.cardGradient)`, `BrandVividType.title`/`body`, and a `VividButton` "Tower" that calls `context.go(Routes.tower())`. The ground is `VividBackdrop(grade: pressure)` (:82). The header is `VividHeader` with a `ProgressRing` (accent, 52px) showing the streak (:113-145).

**Card** (`recall_card_view.dart`):
- `VividPanel` with grade lobby for trap and pressure for lesson (:51-53).
- Text: `q.kicker` (`BrandVividType.kicker`), the prompt (`title`, quoted for traps), "It led you to: “quote”" (italic `body`), then `q.ask` (`row`).
- Choices are `AnswerCard` (`ui/choice/answer_card.dart`) with idle/locked/focused/disabled states (:39-46), wrapped in `StaggeredEntrance`.
- After answering: the verdict line (accent if known, otherwise `onDeep`), the takeaway, and a `VividButton` "Next"/"Finish" (:109-128).
- Callbacks: `onAnswer` → `RecallRoomController.answer` (:194), `onNext` → `next()` (:243).

**Data model**
- `RecallQuestion` (`recall_deck.dart:27-59`): `cardId`, `kind` (`RecallCardKind.trap|lesson`, :7-15), `kicker`, `prompt` (the tempting misconception or the lesson's question), `quote` (what it led to), `ask`, `choices`, `correctChoiceId`, `takeaway`.
- It has **no box, due day, source mission or interviewer.** Those live only in the engine's `RecallCard(cardId, box: LeitnerBox.one|three|seven, due: CalendarDay)` (`packages/engine/lib/src/recall.dart:6-42`) and are not passed to the view.

**Analytics**
- `recall_card_answered` fires at `recall_controller.dart:228-232` with `card_kind`, `verdict` and `box`.
- `recall_room_opened` fires at :182, and `recall_session_completed` at :256.

**Due count and dates**
- `recallDueCountProvider` (:53-73) returns only a count. It is consumed by `features/tower/tower_extras.dart:55`, `welcome_back_card.dart:76` and `tower_screen.dart:109,196`.
- There is **no "next due date" API.** The closest is the per-day closure `recallDue: (day) => …` in `lib/core/notifications/reminders.dart:98-99`, plus `dueRecallCards` and `reviewRecallCard` in the engine.

Its commits job only runs on PRs, so I ran the same commit-range check locally; it passed. My last push (status file and session log only) had no CI run of its own; the pre-push hook passed.

**Local gates:** secrets, standards 25/25, format, analyze with no issues, events, env, RLS and the package tests all pass (mobile 1,002, brand 349, content 309, engine 137).

## What changed, per screen

- **Your file** (the Skills tab) is now a light profile screen:
  - **Progress at the top:** a floor 1 green panel with the XP in large type, the weekly ring in honey, the week line and the interview countdown. Under it are three tiles: rooms lit, average, and the change since room 1. A file with nothing played shows "Start room 1".
  - **Skills:** the tower's skill badges with real names (earned, next, locked), plus a "Next: …" card saying what the skill means and where it's earned. The "CI EB CN TUP" chips are gone.
  - **Your four scores:** a new Daylight radar, with an empty state.
  - **Chapter 1 report card:** a white card with a button once it's ready, a sand card with a lock before.
  - **Rooms by floor**, each in its floor's colour, titles in sentence case:
    - A played room is a pressable card showing its latest score, best score, number of tries, and an "AI score" or "Offline score" tag.
    - The next room has a coloured ring; locked rooms are flat sand with a lock.
    - A floor you haven't reached folds into one card listing its rooms.
  - **How you play:** Settings has no such toggles, so these stay here, restyled as Daylight switches.
  - **States:** loading skeleton, empty, part played, all played, a Daylight error card, and offline (everything is read from the phone).
  - Stars are removed (the direction says no stars).
- **Chapter 1 report:**
  - a floor 1 header with first try → review;
  - one radar with the first try drawn under the review;
  - a bar for each skill;
  - a Daylight "Then and now" card;
  - how to read the scores;
  - the next floor in its own colour ("Take the lift to floor 2").
- **Progression pieces:**
  - The progress card is now the header panel above.
  - The round initials badge is now a small arch medal with the skill's glyph. The debrief's unlock card uses it; I didn't edit that card.
  - The interview date choices are white tap tiles.
- **Title:** Northstar tower in a day sky. Its windows light honey floor by floor, with Sam's window last, then "The Last Room" appears and the four interviewers arrive in arches. The button is "Step inside". The old vivid title is deleted. The wrong-floor screen now shows Priya thinking.
- **Onboarding:**
  - each step on its floor's tint, with a step bar, a quiet Skip and a honey Next;
  - interviewer cards with arch portraits and "First met in room N", plus Sam at dusk;
  - a white name field;
  - kickers in sentence case.
- **Practice CV:** a Daylight sheet with your initial in an arch, and Priya waiting.
- **Epilogue** (look only): the floor's tint, the speaker talking in the room scene, and a Daylight speech bubble. The film letterbox is gone.
- **Your two follow-ups, done:** the film-grain wrap around the epilogue is removed from `mission_player.dart`. The "Then and now" card is now one Daylight widget shared by the room 7 debrief and the report.
- **Merge from #56:** I merged it at adc3861. The one conflict, in `every_mission_test`'s button finder, I resolved with WS2's version.

## How previous results work

A played room's card in Your file opens a new screen (`past_result_screen.dart`). Each attempt is already stored in full on the phone (picks, reasons, voice points, score), so nothing is scored again. The screen:

- shows the **Candidate assessment sheet itself, reused unchanged**, with the same stamps, red pen and seal (spoken rooms use the voice debrief's own red-pen rule), placed on the floor's colour;
- shows the AI's reason when the AI scored that try, otherwise "Offline score";
- compares latest and best for each dimension;
- shows the lesson still to work on;
- shows what you said: your lines in quotation marks, or which voice points you made and missed;
- ends with "Play room N again".

A room you haven't played, a number that isn't a room, and a store error each have their own screen. Tests for this are in `past_result_test.dart`.

## The task: contextual first-time tips, not a full tour (the owner approved this)
The tower tour (`apps/mobile/lib/features/tower/tower_tour.dart`, events `tower_tour_shown`/`tower_tour_done`, the Settings row "Show the tower tour again") is tip 1. Generalise it into a small tips system and add the rest. Each tip: one short sentence in an interviewer's voice (cast arch + speech bubble, Daylight kit, the spotlight cut-out where there's a clear target), shown ONCE the first time the player reaches that moment, dismissed with one tap ("Got it" or tapping outside; tapping the highlighted target does its normal action too). Store the flags on-device and offline, the way the tour flag is stored.

| Tip id | When | Copy (keep it short; tune it if needed) |
|---|---|---|
| tower_door | first tower visit | (existing) "This is your first room. Tap the door to start your interview." |
| answer_cards | first answer cards in a choice room | "Pick the line you'd really say, then lock it in." |
| why_step | first "Why that…?" step | "Now tell me why. This is where the points are." |
| first_result | first debrief | a bubble BESIDE or BELOW the Candidate assessment sheet, never on it: "Your weakest skill is underlined in red. Tap 'How we scored this' to see why." Show it only AFTER the sheet's whole slide/stamp/seal/pen timeline has finished. |
| voice_mic | first voice room's recorder | "Tap to talk. I'll pause if you go quiet for 5 seconds." |
| recall_due | first time a Recall note is due | a badge/bubble on the Recall tab: "Priya left you a note. Two minutes." |
| your_file | back on the tower after the first finished room | point at the Skills tab: "Every room you play is kept here." |

Rules:
- Only one overlay at a time. Never over the welcome-back card or the reminder explainer, and never during a timed answer countdown (pause the countdown or show the tip before the timer starts; calm mode exists, read how the timer works). Queue the tips sensibly.
- Never block progress: if a target isn't on screen, skip or defer the tip.
- Reduced motion and 2x text must work (the tour already handles 2x; reuse that).
- Settings: rename "Show the tower tour again" to "Show tips again", which resets ALL tips and returns to the tower.
- Analytics: register before use, in the `AnalyticsEvent` enum + docs/EVENTS.md (non-negotiable 2). Prefer generalising to `tip_shown` {tip, replay} and `tip_done` {tip, action}, replacing tower_tour_*. If you replace events, update every reference and EVENTS.md consistently. No PII, no answer text.
- **ABSOLUTE OWNER RULE:** the Candidate assessment sheet (`apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper/**`, its fonts, tokens, timing and haptics) stays byte-identical, and the goldens paper-dossier.png and paper-seal-stamp-pen.png stay out of the diff. The first_result tip sits outside the sheet and appears after its animation.
- Brand tokens only, no raw colours outside packages/brand, no stock Material look.
- Tests: each tip shows once, dismisses, doesn't show again, the reset brings them all back, only one at a time, no tip during the timed countdown, and the first_result tip waits for the sheet's timeline. Make sure the existing every-mission/campaign tests still pass: tips must not break automated playthroughs (dismiss them in test helpers, or give tests a way to start with tips seen).

## First-time tips are on PR #57 (branch `feat/48-daylight-everywhere`, head 7ab24f5). The CI run passed, the APK is on the realme, and nothing is merged.

The tower tour is now the first of seven tips. Each is one short line from an interviewer in a speech bubble, shown once the first time the player reaches that moment. Where there's a clear target, the screen dims around a honey-ringed cut-out. Tapping the target lets the tap through, so it does its normal action and closes the tip. "Got it" or a tap anywhere else also closes it. The seen flags are stored on the phone and work offline. Anyone who already saw the old tour counts as having seen the door tip.

| Tip | Where | Line |
|---|---|---|
| tower_door | the door to play | "This is your first room. Tap the door to start your interview." |
| answer_cards | the first beat's cards plus "Lock in" | "Pick the line you'd really say, then lock it in." |
| why_step | the reasons, once she has asked why | "Now tell me why. This is where the points are." |
| first_result | a bubble under the Candidate assessment, only after its whole timeline; "How we scored this" gets a honey ring | "Your weakest skill is underlined in red. Tap “How we scored this” to see why." |
| voice_mic | the record button, once the recorder is ready | "Tap to talk. I'll pause if you go quiet for 5 seconds." |
| recall_due | the Recall tab | **Changed:** "I left you a note in Recall. Two minutes." Priya says it, so "Priya left you a note" read oddly. |
| your_file | the Skills tab, back on the tower after the first room | "Every room you play is kept here." |

- **One tip at a time:** only one tip can be on screen anywhere in the app, and the tower shows at most one per visit. The order there is door, then Skills, then Recall.
- **Never on top of other things:**
  - No tip over the welcome-back card.
  - No tip over the reminder explainer: the Skills tip waits until it is answered.
  - No tip over the notice above the tab bar.
- **Timed rooms:** the beat timer pauses while a tip is up.
- **Missing target:** if the target isn't on screen, the tip is skipped until its moment comes again.
- **Tall targets** (four answer cards, or 2x text): the cut-out shrinks so it starts below the bubble rather than sitting under it.
- **Reduced motion and 2x text:** with reduced motion the tip just appears. At 2x the bubble scrolls, so "Got it" can always be reached.
- **Voice debrief:** there is no first_result tip there, because that screen has no "How we scored this" button.
- **Settings:** the row is now "Show tips again". It resets every tip and returns to the tower.
- **Automated playthroughs:** the mission and voice test helpers start with every tip marked as seen. All 1,029 mobile tests pass, including every-mission and campaign.

## The owner's ask
"Can we do some animations on the back button as well, like when we enter a screen and come back with the back button? That would also be attractive and give the app a premium look."

## Build (Daylight motion tokens only: DaylightMotion durations/curves; no magic numbers)
1. **Route transitions in `apps/mobile/lib/app/router.dart`**: one shared Daylight page-transition builder (a new file under ui/daylight/, e.g. `daylight_route.dart`), used by every GoRoute through `pageBuilder`/`CustomTransitionPage`.
   - **Push:** a shared-axis horizontal motion. The new screen slides in about 24–32 dp from the trailing edge while fading in; the old screen drifts slightly left and dims. About 300 ms, DaylightMotion.ease.
   - **Back:** the exact reverse, so leaving feels like stepping back out.
   - **Tab switches** in the floating tab bar (Tower / Recall / Skills / You): a fade-through (out, then in with a slight scale from 0.98).
   - **Entering a room from a tower door:** a signature "through the door" transition. The door's arch expands into the room (a container transform from the tapped door's rect, or an arch-shaped clip reveal that grows from the door), and on exit the room shrinks back into its door. This is the premium moment, so make it feel crafted, but at 450 ms or less.
   - **Modal sheets** (floor sheet, choice sheets, How we scored this): a slide up with a slight overshoot and the scrim fading in.
   - **Android predictive back:** support Flutter 3.47's predictive back gesture (PredictiveBackPageTransitionsBuilder or an equivalent hook, and `android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback` if needed) so the swipe-back previews the screen behind, Android 14+, with a graceful fallback on the owner's realme RMX3085 (it may be Android 13 or lower; check with `adb -s LFCQ8HKNAUDIFUPR shell getprop ro.build.version.release`).
   - **Reduced motion** (`MediaQuery.disableAnimations` / the app's reduced-motion setting): a 150 ms cross-fade or none.
2. **Back controls:** every back/close control (the header "‹ Tower" pill, the ✕ in the mission top bar, sheet close buttons) uses the kit's Tactile press. On tap the chevron nudges left (about 4 dp, 120 ms) before the pop, with a light haptic (haptics.pressTap). The system back button and the gesture give the same transition as the on-screen control. Keep `back_to_exit.dart`'s behaviour (double back to exit on the root), restyled if it shows a toast: a Daylight snack bubble, not a Material SnackBar.
3. **Hard owner rule:** the debrief's Candidate assessment sheet (apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper/**: its slide-in, stamps, seal, pen, timing, haptics) stays byte-identical, and the goldens paper-dossier.png and paper-seal-stamp-pen.png stay out of the diff. Entering the debrief route should only fade in the ground (≤ 200 ms) so the sheet's own timeline plays exactly as before. Don't delay or overlap it with a slide.
4. **No regression:** the mission player's in-room beats, the voice recorder, the welcome-back card, the reminder explainer and the tower tour must work as before. Tests that pump routes may need `pumpAndSettle` adjustments; keep them meaningful.

## Brief 50: route transitions and back controls are done, PR #58 is open and CI is green

**PR:** https://github.com/samiarif02/thelastroom/pull/58. Its base is now `main`, as you asked. Branch `feat/50-transitions` at 0742003 in `/Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w16`. Not merged, not installed on the phone.
**CI:** run 41604900840 (pull_request on 0742003) passed all four jobs: gates/tests/web build, landing page, commits, migrations + RLS. An earlier run I dispatched on the old base (41603045341) is stale and I cancelled it.

### Merge with main
- I merged `origin/main` at 5fde7e2 (no rebase), which brought in #56, #57 and the tips.
- There was one real conflict, in `TowerScreen.didChangeDependencies`. I kept both sides: my lift reset after a room, and the tips' `_tipThisVisit = false`.
- **Tips now wait for the screen to finish moving.** A new `routeSettled(context)` completes once the route has arrived and nothing is moving over it. The tower's `_placeTip`, the mission's `_offerTip` and the voice mic tip all wait on it before measuring their target, so no tip appears mid-transition.
- The spotlight's position was already right before this change, because the tip and its target sit inside the same screen.
- There's a test for this, and the full suite passed on the merged tree.

### What each transition does
All timings and curves come from `DaylightMotion` tokens. I added these to `packages/brand`, with tests: `page` 300 ms, `door` 450 ms, `ground` 200 ms, `reducedPage` 150 ms, `nudge` 120 ms, `settle` (about 3% overshoot) and `swing` (a symmetric ease-in-out).

| Where | Arrival | Back |
|---|---|---|
| **Push** (report, practice CV) | Slides in 32 dp from the trailing edge while fading in. The screen below drifts 16 dp the other way and dims. 300 ms. | The exact reverse, slowing into place |
| **Tabs** (Recall / Skills / You) and roots | Fade-through: the old screen fades to the paper ground, then the new one fades in from 0.98 scale | Reverse |
| **Room from a door** | The lift doors stay shut. An arch grows from the tapped door's rect with a honey rim, and the room scales up from 0.9 around the door. The tower leans in and dims. 450 ms. | The room shrinks back into its door |
| **Past result (Candidate assessment) and floor cleared** | Only a 200 ms fade, nothing slides, so the sheet's own timeline plays as before | Fade |
| **Sheets** (floor sheet, How we scored this) | Slide up with a slight overshoot while the scrim fades in. A white strip under the sheet hides any gap during the overshoot. | Eases out, no bounce |
| **Reduced motion** | Every route is a 150 ms cross-fade | Same |

- **Door origin:**
  - If a door was tapped, the room opens from that door's rect.
  - The up-next button and the tour use the next room's door, if it's on screen.
  - Anything else opens from a door in the middle of the screen.
- **Back controls:** every top-bar back or ✕ tile (including the mission ✕) and the sheet close tiles nudge their icon 4 dp toward the way out over 120 ms, with a light haptic, then act.
  - A second tap during the nudge is ignored.
  - Under reduced motion they act at once.
- **System back:** the hardware back button and the gesture play the same reverse transition as the on-screen control.
- **Exit hint:** `back_to_exit.dart` keeps its double-back-to-exit behaviour. The hint is now a Daylight ink bubble with a honey door icon.
- **Not given the nudge:** labelled arrow buttons like "Back to the tower" in the report and past result. Changing them would have meant editing those screens.

### Predictive back
- On Android 14+, the back swipe scrubs the route's own reverse transition: the screen behind is previewed, and a cancelled swipe springs back.
- Screens that ask before leaving (the mission, via `PopScope(canPop: false)`) keep their leave sheet.
- I set `android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback="true"` in the manifest.
- **The owner's realme RMX3085 is Android 13 (API 33)**, checked with adb getprop. There is no in-app swipe preview on that version, so back there runs the normal pop with the same reverse transition.

### Files changed
- **New:**
  - `apps/mobile/lib/ui/daylight/daylight_route.dart`
  - `apps/mobile/test/ui/daylight_route_test.dart`
  - `apps/mobile/test/app/stills/route_frames_test.dart` and `flutter_test_config.dart`
  - `docs/status/50.md`
  - `.pr-screenshots/50/*.png`
- **Changed in the app:**
  - `app/router.dart`: every route except the dev ones goes through `daylightPage`.
  - `app/back_to_exit.dart`
  - `ui/daylight/icon_tile.dart`, `top_bar.dart`, `daylight_sheet.dart`
  - `features/debrief/scoring_basis_sheet.dart`: its transition only.
  - `features/tower/northstar_tower.dart`: the door reports its rect when tapped.
  - `features/tower/tower_screen.dart`: passes the door to the route, keeps the lift shut while the room opens, and waits before placing tips.
  - `features/mission/mission_player.dart` and `features/voice/voice_player.dart`: tips wait for the room to settle.
- **Other:**
  - `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`
  - `packages/brand/lib/src/daylight.dart` and its test
- **Tests I adjusted, still meaningful:**
  - The leave-sheet tests in completeness, playthrough_fixes and voice_mission now pump the nudge before expecting the sheet.
  - `conversation_test` taps Resume once the room's door has finished opening.
  - In `every_mission_test`, the floor-cleared screen and the report both have a "Take the lift to floor 2" button. The test now waits for the floor-cleared screen to leave before tapping the report's.

### Proof
- **Candidate assessment untouched:** `git diff f54b5ed HEAD -- apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper` is empty, and `paper-dossier.png` and `paper-seal-stamp-pen.png` are not in the diff.
- **Goldens:** I redrew all 59 catalog goldens in Linux Docker on Flutter 3.47.8 (SDK sha256 baf7dcc5…, matching releases_linux.json). All 59 came out byte-identical.
- **Local gates:**
  - Before the merge: secrets, standards 25/25, format, analyze, events, env, RLS, `npm run test`, build:web and the commit range all passed.
  - On the merged tree, `npm run test` passed: mobile 1,054, brand 352, content 309, engine 137, gate scripts 65. Format, analyze, standards, events, env, secrets and the commit range also passed.
- **Frame strips** in `.pr-screenshots/50/`, recorded from widget-test frame captures at 360×800 and each one looked at: push-report, back-report, tab-recall, tab-back, door-in, door-out, door-reduced, sheet-floor, exit-hint.

The CI run ID is recorded in `docs/status/50.md` in the working tree, but that edit is uncommitted. I didn't push it, to avoid another CI run.

## The owner asked for three things
"Check all features are working or not, also complete the first chapter on my phone so I can test the Chapter 2 voice thing you developed. Also, is the AI scoring working or not?"

### 1. Live AI scoring on the phone (do this first, so the Chapter 1 rooms you play are AI-scored)
Today the phone build has no backend, so results say "OFFLINE SCORE". Set up the local backend:
- In w17: `supabase start` (Docker is running), so migrations apply.
- Serve the Edge Functions with the Gemini key file WITHOUT reading it: `supabase functions serve --env-file /Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom/.env`. Run it detached so it outlives you (`nohup … > <scratchpad>/functions.log 2>&1 &`), and record its PID and how to stop it in your status file. NEVER cat, grep, print or copy that .env or any real env file (.claude/settings.json denies it, and this is a hard rule).
- `adb -s LFCQ8HKNAUDIFUPR reverse tcp:54321 tcp:54321`, so the phone reaches the Mac's local Supabase at 127.0.0.1:54321 over USB.
- Write a define file OUTSIDE the repo, in the scratchpad /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-mac-WebstormProjects-thelastroom-w4/16b13504-57b3-4433-8f94-5e628ec1ba58/scratchpad/phone-local.json, shaped like apps/mobile/env/dev.example.json, with SUPABASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:54321 and SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = the LOCAL anon key from `supabase status -o env` (it's the public local-dev key, safe in the app per the secrets rule; there's no service-role key in the app, ever). Leave the other values empty.
- `cd apps/mobile && flutter build apk --release --dart-define-from-file=<that file>`, then `adb install -r`, then launch.
- Prove it works on the phone: play a choice room, and its result must show the AI's verdict (no "Offline score" chip) and the "How we scored this" sheet with the AI's reasoning. Check functions.log for the `score` call and its model, and quote the line in your report (no keys). If it falls back to offline, debug it: auth/guest sign-in, the reverse port, CORS, a Gemini 429. Remember the app gives up at 8 s and accepts a late verdict up to 30 s. Gemini stays on the free tier; pace the requests.
- Note the caveat in your report: live AI only works while the phone is on USB to this Mac with Supabase running.

### 2. Finish Chapter 1 on the phone
Play the remaining Floor 1 rooms (through room 7) on the real phone, through the UI, with adb (`adb exec-out screencap -p` to see, `adb shell input tap x y` to act; the screen is 1080×2400 px). Play well, so the owner's file shows sensible scores: choose the strongest, specific, honest lines and the matching reasons. Go through room 7's epilogue and the Chapter 1 report, so floor 2 opens. Confirm the tower shows floor 2 unlocked with its next door ajar. The first-time tips (door, cards, why, first result, Skills, Recall) may appear; dismiss them normally. Make sure the **voice_mic tip stays unseen**, because the owner should meet it in Chapter 2: don't open any voice room and don't press "Show tips again". Leave the app on the tower with the floor 2 door showing.

### 3. Check that every feature works (a smoke test, on the phone)
While playing and afterwards, check each item and record pass/fail with a screenshot (in the scratchpad, named qa-*.png):
- the title → tower; the weekly ring and XP;
- the floor sheets (tap floor 2 and floor 3);
- door states; the tower tip;
- the answer cards; lock-in; the timed beat (and calm mode in Your file → How you play);
- the why step (never stuck: the earlier P0); the feedback sheet;
- the debrief: the Candidate assessment sheet animating (slide, stamps, seal, pen), the Daylight ground, "How we scored this", the XP, the line that cost the most, reasoning;
- the room 7 epilogue; the Chapter 1 report; the floor-cleared reward screen;
- Your file: past results open the stored assessment; rooms grouped by floor; skills medals;
- Recall Room (a note if one is due, or the empty state);
- Settings: reminders, "Send a test reminder" (it should arrive), "Show tips again" (DON'T press it, just check it's there);
- leave a room mid-way and resume it; the back button/gesture;
- airplane mode during a choice room's result: the offline score still appears, and no crash.
- Voice (Chapter 2) readiness, WITHOUT opening a voice room: confirm the `score-voice` function is served (functions.log lists it), and that the RECORD_AUDIO permission is in the manifest.

## Report back
Report AI scoring (working or not, with the evidence), Chapter 1 completion (which rooms you played, their scores, floor 2 open), the feature table (pass/fail plus screenshots), each bug found (steps, suspected file), and the exact state you leave things in: the Supabase containers, the functions-serve PID and how to stop it, adb reverse, and which build is on the phone (commit + define file). ## Phone QA report (realme RMX3085, worktree w17 at 5fde7e2)

All findings are in `/Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w17/docs/status/51-phone-qa.md`, which is untracked and not committed. Screenshots are `qa-*.png` in `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-mac-WebstormProjects-thelastroom-w4/16b13504-57b3-4433-8f94-5e628ec1ba58/scratchpad/`.

### The biggest finding: a P0 bug that blocked AI scoring on every installed APK
- The release manifest has no `android.permission.INTERNET`. The file is `apps/mobile/android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`; INTERNET is only in the `src/debug` and `src/profile` manifests.
- `aapt dump permissions` on w15's release APK confirmed it, and so did `dumpsys package` on the build that was on the phone.
- Effect: no release APK can reach the network, whatever env file it was built with. That covers AI scoring, Chapter 2 voice scoring, sync, sign-in and the waitlist. The offline fallback hides the failure.
- To test on the phone, I added the INTERNET line locally in w17. It is uncommitted, so the lead needs to put the fix through a real commit and PR.

### 1. AI scoring works on the phone, with that fix
- The local stack is up, migrations applied, and guest sign-in landed in `auth.users`. The outbox synced the owner's attempts.
- Every AI-scored room showed "Scored by AI · Senior recruiter". The "How we scored this" sheet shows "Recruiter 90 · Game 85" plus the AI's reasoning for each dimension.
- Each verdict has a row in `public.ai_scores`. Room 5's line from functions.log:
  `{"fn":"score","event":"ai_score","model":"gemini-3.5-flash-lite","mission":"m05_something_went_wrong","prompt_tokens":639,"output_tokens":215,"ms":1897}`
- Rooms 6, 7 and 2 have matching lines (1.9–2.1 s each). There were no 429 or 503 errors.
- **Caveat:** live AI only works while the phone is on USB to this Mac with Supabase and the functions running. Otherwise rooms get the offline score and voice rooms get the self-check.

### 2. Chapter 1 is complete
| Room | How it was played | Score |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 5 | The owner's half-played attempt, continued | AI 90, engine 85 |
| 6 | Fresh | AI 85 |
| 7 | Own-words draft skipped, since those words should be the owner's | AI 82, engine 98 |

- I went through the room 7 epilogue, the floor-cleared screen and the Chapter 1 report (first try 39 → review 98).
- I missed the floor-cleared screenshot; its content is recorded from the UI tree.
- The owner also had half-played rooms 2 and 3 waiting, which I finished:
  - Room 2: AI 92.
  - Room 3: offline 98, played during the airplane-mode test.
  - Room 3's replay earned Clear Introductions.
- The tower now shows floor 2 open (0 of 7) with door 8 ajar, Lena looking out and the play cue. Floor 1 is 7/7 and XP is 810.
- The app is left on the tower. I never opened a voice room, never pressed "Take the lift" (it opens room 8, a voice room) and never pressed "Show tips again", so the voice_mic tip is still unseen.

### 3. Feature smoke test
Everything passed except the bugs below; the full table with screenshots is in the status file. Items covered:
- Doors, floor sheets, the weekly ring (it counts days) and XP.
- Cards, lock-in, built beats, the timer running out to 0 with nothing forced, and calm mode (no timer line). Calm mode is switched back off.
- The why step, the feedback sheet, and the debrief: slide, stamps, seal, and the pen on room 4's past result.
- "How we scored this", XP, "What cost you" and the reasoning section.
- The epilogue, the report, and Your file: past results open the stored assessment, rooms are grouped by floor, skill medals show.
- The Recall Room: I answered one note and it moved up a box.
- Settings: "Send a test reminder" arrived as "Recall Room · 14 traps are waiting…", and "Show tips again" is present.
- Leaving and resuming a room, twice, and the back button.

Find the root cause by reading the resume path and the stored attempt shape (an attempt saved by an older build/schema may resume with mismatched beat/reason state). Write a failing test that reproduces it, then fix it so a resumed room never credits a reason the player didn't pick in this run, and never skips the why step. If it can't be reproduced, harden the resume path (validate stored state, drop stale reasons) with tests, and say so.
3. **BUG-3 (P3):** the first-result tip says "Your weakest skill is underlined in red" even when nothing is underlined (`features/tips/tips.dart:43`). Use that copy only when the sheet really draws the red pen, otherwise something true ("Tap 'How we scored this' to see how each score was reached."). Don't touch ui/paper/**.
4. **BUG-4 (P2):** in Your file, "Latest" comes from the AI and "Best" from the engine (room 5: Best 85 · 1 try next to latest 90) (`features/progress/progress_screen.dart` ~656–661 and the past-result screen). Make latest and best use the same source per attempt (the shown score: AI when it was AI-scored, offline otherwise), so best ≥ latest always holds for 1 try. Add tests.
5. **Minor:**

## Phone QA fixes: PR #59 is open with CI green, not merged

**PR:** https://github.com/samiarif02/thelastroom/pull/59 (branch `fix/52-phone-qa`, worktree w17, based on origin/main 5fde7e2).

**CI:** all green: commits, "Gates, tests & web build", "Migrations & RLS tests", "Landing page (web/)" and Vercel. Nothing in w16 was touched, and the Supabase stack, PID 65744 and the phone were left alone.

**Commits**
1. `a807436` P0 manifest fix, on its own. The manifest change is only the INTERNET line and its comment, so it should merge cleanly with #58.
2. `d40c52d` BUG-2/3/4 and the minor fixes.
3. `61ca6b2` `docs/status/51-phone-qa.md`, with a "Fixed" section added.
4. Session logs.

The P0 commit's message was amended before the push and now carries the whole PR's `Changelog:` line (the first one wins); the other commits say `Changelog: None`. Nothing was force-pushed or rebased.

## Root cause and fix for each bug

- **P0 / BUG-1 (no INTERNET in release builds):** Flutter's template only declares INTERNET in the `src/debug` and `src/profile` manifests. Every release APK therefore failed every network call, and the offline fallback hid it.
  - **Fix:** the permission is declared in `src/main` with an explanatory comment.
  - **Guard:** a new `check:standards` check, `android-release-permissions`, fails when the main manifest lacks INTERNET. A commented-out line doesn't count. It runs in CI's check job.
  - The PR names it as the fix for "release APKs silently fell back to offline scoring; AI scoring, voice scoring, sync and sign-in never worked in release builds".

- **BUG-2 (resume skipped the why step, credited an old reason):** I couldn't reproduce it, and the screenshots contradict the QA's guess.
  - **What the pips show:** right after Continue (`qa-04`) trust shows 3 pips, which is trust 50. That means the resumed attempt had no lines at all; any open line with a reason gives 42–62.
  - After locking C (the strongest line) it shows 2 pips (`qa-06`), which is 48: exactly "strongest line + the Labelling reason".
  - So the reason was picked in this session, on the why step's reason cards, without being noticed. The likeliest cause is the first-time why-step tip: a tap on its spotlit area goes through to the reason card underneath (by design), and that tip shows only once, which fits "seen once". This is a hypothesis; the tip behaviour is unchanged.
  - **Hardening anyway, tested to fail before the fix:**
    - A save whose beat asks why but has no reason, followed by later lines (saved before why steps existed), now resumes at that why step and drops the later lines.
    - A reason saved on a built beat is dropped.
    - `lock`/`lockBuilt` now return whether they locked anything. If the screen is out of step with the attempt and the lock is rejected, the screen goes to the beat or why step the attempt is really on, instead of reacting to the old line and skipping ahead.

- **BUG-3 (tip promised a red underline):** the tip text was fixed. It now uses the underline wording only when the pen marks a row; otherwise it says "Tap “How we scored this” to see how each score was reached."

- **BUG-4 (Best below Latest in Your file):** "Latest" used the AI's score while "Best" used only the game's own scores.
  - **Fix:** a new `shownTotals` takes every try as it was shown: the AI's score for the latest when the AI scored it, the game's for the earlier ones (only the latest AI verdict is stored).
  - The past result's per-skill "Best" works the same way, so best ≥ latest always holds.

## Minor fixes
- **Built beats:** "Lock it in" is pinned in the dock, and the dock keeps clear of the gesture bar. The reaction no longer shows reason cards, and a strong build reads "Every piece, in order." instead of "That's the principle."
- **Recall Room:** the week note now sits below the list, so it never covers pick C.
- **Recall tip:** it uses the card's count and time ("I left you 15 notes in Recall. About 8 minutes."), from one shared `recallMinutes` estimate.
- **Reminders:** they and the reminder primer say "notes", not "traps".
- **Name:** `PlayerProfile.displayName` now trims trailing punctuation through one helper, `spokenName`, which replaces the three local regexes. The report's kicker reads "Sami", not "Sami.".

## Tests added
- `scripts/check-standards.test.mjs`: the manifest check, including a commented-out permission.
- `apps/mobile/test/features/mission/resume_hardening_test.dart`: 3 widget tests, all failing before the fix.
- `apps/mobile/test/features/progress/latest_best_test.dart`: 4 `shownTotals` tests, a past-result best test and a Your file widget test.
- Additions to existing files:
  - `tips_test.dart`: the clean-run first-result tip, and the Recall tip matching the card.
  - `build_answer_test.dart`: "Lock it in" above a 48 dp gesture bar, and no why under a built beat's reaction.
  - `recall_screen_test.dart`: no pick under the week note, at rest or scrolled; fails before the fix.
  - `reminder_plan_test.dart`: every reminder says "note", never "trap".
  - `profile_repository_test.dart`: name trimming.
- Existing copy tests were updated for "notes" (`reminder_outlook_test.dart`, `welcome_back_test.dart`).

## Local checks
- **Passed locally:** secrets, standards (26/26), format, analyze (no issues), events, env, RLS, gate-script tests (67), the brand/content/engine package tests, the app's `flutter test` (1043 passed) and `build:web`.
- **Not run locally:**
  - The db job: the owner's stack runs from this worktree, so I didn't run db reset. No migration changed, and CI's db job passed.
  - `test:functions` and the landing page: unchanged, and CI's landing-page job passed.
  - Docker goldens: no catalog widget changed, `ui/paper/**` and its goldens aren't in the diff, and CI passed.

## Open items for the lead
- **Screenshots:** the PR has no after-screenshots yet, because I don't install on the phone. They need adding from the lead's phone build before merge.
- **Not merged:** waiting for the owner's "merge #59".

timestamp: 2026-11-03T11:36:25.516Z
time: 2026-11-03 11:36
chars: 4578

You are a worker for The Last Room (Flutter interview-practice game; submission Thu 5 Nov 2026). Work ONLY in /Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w19, branch `feat/54-share-floor-card` (from origin/main 2233f80). Read AGENTS.md, docs/rules/* (code-quality, components, analytics, state-management, error-handling, secrets, commits, pull-requests), docs/design/direction.md (the reward section §6 and "Share your floor card"), docs/status/46.md and 48.md. Status file: docs/status/54.md (never docs/HANDOFF.md). Two other workers run in parallel: w20 (XP for explained reasons: engine, the feedback sheet, debrief XP) and w21 (resume bug + perf). Keep to your files: features/progression/ floor-cleared screen, a new share module, pubspec, the Android/iOS config needed for sharing.

## Build: "Share your floor card"
On the floor-cleared reward screen (route /tower/floor/:n, features/progression/), add the text link/button "Share your floor card" (from direction.md §6). It renders a crafted share card image offscreen: a 1080×1350 (4:5) PNG in Daylight Tower style. It shows the floor's colour with rays, the Northstar tower motif with that floor's doors lit, "Floor N cleared · 7 of 7 rooms lit", the floor's title, the skill medal(s) earned on that floor, then-vs-now (first room → last room overall score on that floor), total XP, the three interviewers of the floor in arches, and a small footer "The Last Room · thelastroom-ten.vercel.app". It then opens the OS share sheet with the image plus a one-line text ("I cleared floor 1 of The Last Room: Get through the door. thelastroom-ten.vercel.app").
- Use `share_plus` (a current version compatible with Flutter 3.47.8, Dart 3.x; check that it builds for Android, iOS and web, since CI builds web; on web, fall back to downloading or sharing the PNG via share_plus web support, or hide the button if unsupported) and `path_provider` if needed for the temp file. Run `flutter pub get` in the pub workspace. Keep the license and secrets checks green.
- Render via RepaintBoundary/`toImage` at pixelRatio for 1080 wide, or a PictureRecorder, from a widget built with the Daylight kit and brand tokens only (no raw colours). Use the bundled fonts; make sure they're loaded before rendering.
- Analytics: register `floor_card_shared` {floor, outcome: shared/dismissed/unavailable} in the `AnalyticsEvent` enum + docs/EVENTS.md FIRST, in its own commit. No PII: no player name on the card unless they typed one; then use the trimmed `spokenName`, and say so in the PR, since a name on a shared image is the player's own choice. Default to showing the name only as an initial in an arch? Choose: show the player's first name only if set, and note it.
- Errors: if share fails or isn't available, show a calm Daylight bubble ("Couldn't open sharing. Try again.") per docs/rules/error-handling.md; log through the `log` wrapper, never print.
- Reduced motion: no animation needed on the card itself.
- Also offer the same share from the Chapter 1 report's next-floor area if trivial; otherwise skip it and say so.
- Tests: the card widget renders for floors 1–3 (a golden of the card at a small size, drawn on Linux in Docker per the repo's method: Flutter 3.47.8, SDK sha256 checked against releases_linux.json, the storage.flutter-io.cn mirror if Google storage is slow; look at the images), the button is present, the share is invoked through an injectable sharer (a fake in tests), the event fires, and the failure path shows the bubble.
- The Candidate assessment sheet (apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper/**) and its goldens (paper-dossier.png, paper-seal-stamp-pen.png) must not change.

## Process
Conventional Commits (≤ 72 char headers; `Changelog:` line for user-facing). Never force-push or rebase. Run the `local-review` skill. Run long jobs with run_in_background and poll them; a watchdog kills a worker that's silent for 600 s. Logs right before each push: `CLAUDE_TRANSCRIPT=$(realpath ~/.claude/projects/-Users-mac-WebstormProjects-thelastroom-w2/16b13504-57b3-4433-8f94-5e628ec1ba58.jsonl) CLAUDE_CWD=/Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w19 python3 .claude/hooks/extract-log.py </dev/null`, `git add .claude-logs`, commit `chore(claude): add session logs`, then confirm `git status` is clean. Push and open a PR into main with stills (.pr-screenshots/54/, including the rendered share card PNGs). Wait for green CI. DO NOT MERGE. Don't touch the owner's phone (they're testing on it). Never read real env files.

Report: the PR URL, CI, the card images (paths), the package versions added, and the web behaviour.

## Brief 54 "Share your floor card": PR #62 is open, CI is not finished yet

**PR:** https://github.com/samiarif02/thelastroom/pull/62 (branch `feat/54-share-floor-card` into main). It is not merged.

**CI:** I had to hand back before the run finished. When I last checked, only Vercel and Vercel Preview Comments had reported, and both passed. The GitHub Actions jobs (check, web, commits, db) had not reported yet. Please watch them with `gh pr checks 62`. Everything CI runs passed locally except the `db` job, which I left to CI: no migration is touched and the local Supabase is shared with the other worktrees.

**Commits:**
1. `86c16ef feat(events): register floor_card_shared`: the event was registered first, on its own.
2. `c3d3aee feat(mobile): share your floor card…`: the feature, with a `Changelog:` line.
3. `chore(claude): add session logs`: committed last, right before the push. `git status` is clean.

### What was built
- **The link.** "Share your floor card" sits under the buttons on the floor-cleared screen (`/tower/floor/:n`). It is also under the Chapter 1 report's next-floor card, which was a one-line change, so I included it.
- **The card.** A tap draws a 1080 × 1350 PNG off screen and opens the system share sheet with it and the line "I cleared floor N of The Last Room: <title>. thelastroom-ten.vercel.app". The card shows:
  - the floor colour with rays and the kicker "Floor N cleared · 7 of 7 rooms lit"
  - the floor's title
  - a small Northstar tower with this floor's doors lit and outlined
  - the medals earned on that floor
  - then vs now (the first room's first try against the last room's latest, with the gain)
  - total XP
  - the floor's three interviewers in honey arches
  - the footer "The Last Room · thelastroom-ten.vercel.app"
- **Name.** The card shows "Cleared by <first name>" only if the player typed one, using the trimmed `spokenName`. Otherwise there is no name. The PR says so, and the event never carries the name.
- **Floor 3** shows "7/7 rooms lit" where the medal would be. No floor-3 mission in the content can earn a skill yet (`thinkUnderPressure` isn't earnable), so there is no medal to show.
- **Code.** New module `apps/mobile/lib/features/share/`:
  - `floor_card_facts.dart`: the card's facts
  - `floor_card.dart`: the card, brand tokens and the Daylight kit only
  - `card_renderer.dart`: draws the card off screen to a PNG; injectable
  - `card_sharer.dart`: the share interface and its `share_plus` version; injectable
  - `share_floor_card_link.dart`: the link, with "Making your card…" while busy
- **Analytics.** `floor_card_shared` {floor, outcome: shared/dismissed/unavailable} fires on every tap.
- **Errors.** If sharing fails, a calm ink bubble says "Couldn't open sharing. Try again." for 4 s, the error goes to the log through `log.warn`, and the link is ready again.
- **Off limits.** `ui/paper/**` and its goldens are unchanged.

### Packages added
- `share_plus ^13.3.1` (resolved 13.3.1). It brings in `cross_file` 0.3.5+5, `share_plus_platform_interface` 7.2.0, `win32` 6.4.0 and `ffi_leak_tracker` 0.1.2.
- All are BSD-3-Clause.
- I didn't need `path_provider`, because `share_plus` writes the picture to a temp file itself.
- Android needs no config. iOS gets `NSPhotoLibraryAddUsageDescription`, which the share sheet's "Save Image" needs.

### Web behaviour
- In browsers that can share files (Safari, Chrome on Android, Windows and macOS), it uses the browser's own share sheet with the PNG.
- In other browsers the PNG downloads as `the-last-room-floor-N.png`. I turned off share_plus's email fallback.
- The browser gives no answer back, so on the web the event always says `unavailable`. No bubble shows unless sharing actually throws.
- `npm run build:web` passes.

### Card images (I looked at all of them)
- Full size from the real renderer: `/Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w19/.pr-screenshots/54/share-card-floor-1.png`, `-2.png`, `-3.png`
- Screen stills: `/Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w19/.pr-screenshots/54/after-floor-cleared-link.png` and `after-share-failed-bubble.png`
- Goldens drawn on Linux: `/Users/mac/WebstormProjects/thelastroom-w19/apps/mobile/test/features/dev_catalog/goldens/share-card-floor-{1,2,3}.png`
  - Drawn in Docker with Flutter 3.47.8 from storage.flutter-io.cn; the SDK's sha256 `baf7dcc5…0d87dd2` matches releases_linux.json.
  - The 59 existing catalog goldens, the paper ones included, still pass unchanged on Linux.

### Tests and gates (local)
- **Tests.** `test/features/share/share_floor_card_test.dart` covers:
  - the card's facts
  - the link on the floor-cleared screen
  - the share going through a fake sharer, with the right file name and line
  - the event for shared and for dismissed
  - the failure bubble: it shows, it logs, and it clears
  - the trimmed name
  - the real renderer producing a 1080 × 1350 PNG

  There are also stills tests (`WRITE_STILLS=1`) and the golden test.
- **Gates (local-review).** All passed:
  - check:secrets and standards (26/26)
  - format and analyze
  - events (42 registered) and env (only the existing ANTHROPIC_API_KEY warning)
  - RLS
  - `npm run test`: mobile 1,081, brand 352, content 309, engine 137
  - Edge Functions: 36 passed, in Docker on Deno 2.9.8
  - build:web, and the landing page's lint, typecheck and build
  - the commit-message range check
  - `flutter build apk --debug`

  I didn't touch the owner's phone.

### Left to do
- Confirm CI goes green on #62.
- `docs/status/54.md` on the branch still lists "local-review, push, PR" as its next step. I kept that line out of the branch to avoid another CI run; whoever next pushes to it could add a line pointing at PR #62.
- Two small repeats are allowed under the Rule of Three: the card's tower colours copy the tower's, and its rays copy the reward screen's. If a third copy appears, they should be pulled out into one place.

## Build: XP for each explained reason
Today the engine awards XP per room but nothing per reason, so the mockup's "+10 XP" on the feedback sheet was left out (it would have been made up). Make it real:
1. **Engine:** in progression, add a deterministic per-reason XP award. A reason judged sound (the engine's existing reason verdicts: sound / close / guess, or whatever the code calls them) earns a named-constant amount (e.g. sound +10, close +5, guess 0; pick the numbers so a room's total stays in a sensible range against the current room XP, and document the reasoning). A built beat (no why step) earns nothing per reason. Re-locking or resuming must never double-award: the award is a pure function of the attempt's final reasons. Fold it into the room's XP total, so the debrief's "+N XP", Your file's XP, the weekly progress and any unlock thresholds all agree, and show the breakdown (room XP + reason XP) where the debrief shows XP. Check every unlock/threshold that reads XP (skills, floors, rewards): if adding reason XP changes unlock timing, keep the thresholds' intent (scale thresholds or keep reason XP outside the unlock math, and justify the choice). Existing saved attempts: XP must be recomputed consistently from stored attempts (no migration of the Candidate assessment data). If XP is stored rather than derived, handle old rows.
2. **UI:** the feedback sheet after a why step shows a small honey "+10 XP" tag for a sound reason (+5 for close, none for a guess), with a short count-up (DaylightMotion.countUp; reduced motion: static). The debrief's XP line shows the breakdown, e.g. "+62 XP · 40 for the room, 22 for your reasons". Don't touch apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper/** (the Candidate assessment sheet): it and its goldens paper-dossier.png / paper-seal-stamp-pen.png stay byte-identical. The XP line sits outside the sheet.
3. **Voice rooms:** decide whether voice points covered should earn the equivalent (e.g. +5 per point covered). Keep it consistent with the content-only voice scoring, and document it.
4. **Analytics:** if an existing event carries xp, keep its meaning; add a field (e.g. `reason_xp`) only after updating the `AnalyticsEvent` registry + docs/EVENTS.md (non-negotiable 2).
5. **Tests:** engine unit tests (each verdict, built beats, no double-award on resume, totals), property-style checks that XP never decreases, widget tests for the tag and the debrief breakdown, and the every-mission campaign test still green. Update goldens on Linux in Docker per the repo method (Flutter 3.47.8, SDK sha256 checked against releases_linux.json, the storage.flutter-io.cn mirror if slow), looking at each changed image.
6. Update docs/requirements.md / completion.md rows about per-reason XP to "Built".

## Process
Conventional Commits (≤ 72 char headers; `Changelog:` for user-facing; engine first, then UI). Never force-push or rebase. Run the `local-review` skill. I saved the work as you asked: everything is committed and pushed, and PR #61 is open into main, not merged. CI passed on the last commit with code in it (8485c97). The final head, 0a340dc, only adds the status file and session log. Its CI run hadn't shown up when I stopped (only the Vercel check, still pending), so that one still needs a look.

1. **Branch head and PR:** `feat/55-reason-xp` at 0a340dc, https://github.com/samiarif02/thelastroom/pull/61. main (4e68920) is merged in; the only conflict was the shared session log, which I regenerated (only its metadata header differed).
2. **XP numbers:** a "why that line?" answered with the right principle earns +10, a considered wrong one +5, and a guessed line 0, whatever the reason. A built beat (no why step) and attempts saved before wave 5 earn nothing.
   - Why these: a room is worth 20–100 XP, and choice rooms ask why 2–3 times, so reasons add at most 30 (a perfect first clear becomes 130). Close earns half because XP rewards practice and the score rewards being right; picking reasons at random averages 6.7 a step against 10.
   - **Voice rooms:** I decided they earn +5 per point covered. That reads the same points their content-only score uses, and a full answer earns 30–35, level with a choice room's 20–30. Without it the voice rooms, which are harder, would be the worse deal.
3. **Unlocks kept fair:** nothing in the game reads XP. Skills unlock on dimension scores, rooms and floors open by clearing the room before, and the weekly ring counts days. So reason XP goes into every total (debrief, tower, Your file, floor totals) and no unlock moves.
   - XP is worked out from the stored picks, never stored, so a resume or restore can't award twice and old attempts earn what they did. No migration.
   - A reason an older build saved on a built beat keeps its score but earns no XP.
   - No event carried XP, so analytics is unchanged.
4. **UI:** the feedback sheet shows "+10 XP" or "+5 XP", counting up as it pops in (no count under reduced motion), and nothing for a guess. The debrief line reads "+127 XP · 97 for the room, 30 for your reasons" ("your points" in voice rooms) and wraps under the tag at 2x text.
   - `ui/paper/**`, `paper-dossier.png` and `paper-seal-stamp-pen.png` are byte-identical to main.
   - Goldens were redrawn on Linux in Docker (Flutter 3.47.8, SDK sha256 matched releases_linux.json). Only the two feedback sheet images changed, and I looked at both. Before/after stills are in `.pr-screenshots/55/`.
5. **Tests added:** `packages/engine/test/reason_xp_test.dart`, with each verdict, built beats, no double award, totals, and property checks that XP never decreases and a sound reason never earns less. App tests cover the tag, the debrief breakdown (choice, voice, none, 2x) and rescoring stored attempts, and the every-mission campaign test now checks all 21 debriefs' XP.
   - The full local-review passed. The first database run failed because another worktree's local Postgres was already up, and my run then stopped that shared database. If w19 or w21 were using it, they'll need to start it again. Rerun alone, the database tests passed.

## Part 1: get to the bottom of BUG-2
QA saw this once on the owner's phone: resuming a saved room 5 (an attempt saved by an older build) re-showed the open beat's cards; after locking, the why step seemed skipped and the debrief credited a reason ("Labelling each STAR step…") the tester says they never picked. PR #59's analysis: the trust pips imply the reason WAS picked this session, most likely through the first-time why_step tip, whose spotlight passes taps through to the reason card underneath. If so, a single tap meant to dismiss a tip silently commits a reason.
- Reproduce both hypotheses with widget tests:
  (a) A resumed old-schema attempt (build fixtures from the stored attempt shape as older builds wrote it; check git history of the attempt model/store for schema changes).
  (b) The tip tap-through: a tap on the spotlit why-step area picks a reason and dismisses the tip at once, so the player never sees their pick.
- Fix (b) if it's real: tapping the spotlight on a CHOICE target (reason cards, answer cards) should only dismiss the tip and highlight, never commit a pick. The door tip may still start the room, since that's an obvious single action. Then the player picks deliberately.
- Fix anything (a) reveals. Add tests that fail before the fix.

## Part 2: NFR-3 frame timing (prepare now, run on the phone only when the lead says)
- Write an integration test (apps/mobile/integration_test/perf_test.dart, adding `integration_test` from the SDK if missing) that drives the heavy paths with `binding.traceAction`/`watchPerformance`: the title animation, tower scroll, door → room transition, the mission beat with typing + cards, the why step, the debrief with the Candidate assessment's stamp/seal timeline, Your file scroll, and the Recall Room. It writes a summary (average/90th/99th build and raster times, missed frames vs the 16.6 ms budget) via `reportData`, plus a `test_driver/perf_driver.dart` that saves timeline summaries to build/perf/.
- The owner's phone has their real progress in the release app com.thelastroom.app, and they're testing on it now. The perf run must NEVER touch that app: give the profile/perf build its own applicationId (e.g. an `applicationIdSuffix ".perf"` in a dedicated build type or flavor, or a -P gradle property) so it installs side by side, and verify the id before any install. Do NOT run anything on the phone yourself. When the harness is ready, write the exact command in docs/status/56.md and report. The lead runs it when the phone is free.
- Also run the same perf test on a desktop/Chrome target or the widget-level FrameTiming equivalent now, to catch obvious jank (e.g. heavy repaints in the tower doors' pulse, the tip overlay's blur, or route transitions; check RepaintBoundary use and that animations stop when offscreen or reduced motion is on), and fix clear problems. Don't touch apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper/** (the Candidate assessment sheet): it and the goldens paper-dossier.png / paper-seal-stamp-pen.png stay byte-identical.
