FR-3 ("Save your progress") is built and CI is green on PR #68. It's not merged. The one thing outstanding is yours: the local stack needs a restart before the emailed code works end to end.

**PR:** https://github.com/samiarif02/thelastroom/pull/68, branch `feat/60-email-sign-in` in w25. The working tree is clean.

**CI:** all checks pass, including "Migrations & RLS tests". That job starts Supabase from the repo config, so the new `config.toml` and email templates load without errors. Locally, everything in local-review except the db job passes, including all 1,147 mobile tests and the web build. No golden changed, and `apps/mobile/lib/ui/paper/**` is untouched.

**One process note:** the PR first conflicted with main on a session-log file (#66 landed meanwhile), so CI didn't start. I merged `origin/main` into the branch with a normal merge commit (no rebase, no force-push) and regenerated the log.

**The flow:** a new "Save your progress" card is the first card in Settings (the "You" tab).
- **Saving:** the guest enters an email, gets a six-digit code (`auth.updateUser(UserAttributes(email:))`), and enters it (`verifyOTP(type: OtpType.emailChange)`). The user id stays the same, so no data moves and the RLS policies still apply; a test checks this.
- **Signing in on another device:** a "Saved on another device? Sign in" button runs `signInWithOtp(shouldCreateUser: false)`, then `verifyOTP(type: OtpType.email)`.
- **After linking:** the card shows the email and explains how to sign in on another device.
- **States:** no backend (a calm note, nothing to press, offline play unaffected), offline, idle, sending, code sent, verifying, linked. Each failure gets its own written note: invalid email, wrong or expired code, rate-limited, offline mid-flow, an email another account already uses, an email nobody saved to, and an unexpected server answer.
- **Analytics:** `account_linked` (`provider`) now fires. `account_signed_in` (`provider`, `had_progress`) was registered in the enum and `docs/EVENTS.md` in its own commit first.
- **No email leaks:** none goes into any event or log line. A test checks this and the logger's redaction.
- **Stills:** 14 PNGs in `.pr-screenshots/60/`, with real type at 390 × 844 and a 2x-text one. The PR description shows eight of them.

**Config change that needs a stack restart (don't skip):** `supabase/config.toml` now has `[auth.email] enable_confirmations = true`, plus code templates `supabase/templates/email_change.html` and `magic_link.html`.
- I tested the running stack's auth API with curl (no db commands). With confirmations off, `PUT /user {email}` links an anonymous user instantly with no code, so anyone could claim any address. The default templates also mail a link, not a code.
- Until you restart the w17 stack, the codes can't work locally. The app copes with that: an instant link goes straight to "linked".
- After the restart, codes land in Mailpit at http://127.0.0.1:54324.
- A hosted project would need the same two templates in its dashboard, with "Confirm email" on.
- My probes left three throwaway users in the shared local auth table (`[email redacted]` and two anonymous ones). They're harmless.

**How a second-device sign-in merges data** (`apps/mobile/lib/core/sync/account_merge.dart`). It follows the sync's existing rule: attempts are append-only and keyed by `attempt_id`, so the result is a union, never an overwrite.
1. As soon as the code is accepted, before the session switches, one local transaction prepares the device:
   - Attempts this device already synced under its old guest id get new ids. Otherwise the server would skip them, because their old ids belong to the guest's rows.
   - Every attempt is queued for upload.
   - The queued profile is dropped, and the merge is marked pending.
   - The Recall Room's record of which attempts it has already turned into cards is updated to the new ids.
2. The new session's sync worker pulls the account's attempts (insert or skip, like the restore) and its profile.
   - The account's profile wins when it has one; otherwise this device's profile goes up.
   - The outbox then sends this device's attempts to the account.
   - If offline, the merge stays pending and every flush retries.
3. Result: both devices end up with the union of attempts and a single profile, the account's. The old guest's server-side AI verdicts (`ai_scores`) stay behind and aren't merged.

**Docs:** FR-3 is rewritten with acceptance criteria in `docs/requirements.md`. `docs/completion.md` marks FR-3 and phase 08 Built. `docs/rules/state-management.md` gets the account-merge rule. Progress is in `docs/status/60.md`. One gap noted in completion.md: the Chapter 2 waitlist doesn't offer "Save your progress" first yet.

## Build
1. **The dated promise, at the end of every room.** On the debrief, outside the Candidate assessment sheet, in the "line that cost the most" area or just under it: a Daylight card in an interviewer's voice naming the REAL next date from the engine's Leitner/Recall schedule and the next room, e.g. "Priya will bring this line back on Friday. 4. Analytics: register `promise_shown` {kind: recall/next_room} and `first_day_shown` (no PII) in the `AnalyticsEvent` enum + docs/EVENTS.md FIRST, in their own commit.
5. Write docs/retention.md: one page on the activation and day-1/3/5/7/8 loop (what the player sees each day, the mechanism, the events that measure it), to answer the reviewer directly. Link it from docs/SUBMISSION.md.
